cache:
  l2_request.all
       [Counts the total number of L2 Cache accesses. Counts on a per core
        basis. Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x24,period=200003/
  l2_request.hit
       [Counts the number of L2 Cache accesses that resulted in a hit. Counts
        on a per core basis. Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x24,period=200003,umask=2/
  l2_request.miss
       [Counts the number of L2 Cache accesses that resulted in a miss. Counts
        on a per core basis. Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x24,period=200003,umask=1/
  longest_lat_cache.miss
       [Counts the number of cacheable memory requests that miss in the LLC.
        Counts on a per core basis. Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x2e,period=200003,umask=0x41/
  longest_lat_cache.reference
       [Counts the number of cacheable memory requests that access the LLC.
        Counts on a per core basis. Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x2e,period=200003,umask=0x4f/
  mem_bound_stalls.ifetch
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to an instruction
        cache or TLB miss which hit in the L2,LLC,DRAM or MMIO (Non-DRAM).
        Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x34,period=200003,umask=0x38/
  mem_bound_stalls.ifetch_dram_hit
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to an instruction
        cache or TLB miss which hit in DRAM or MMIO (Non-DRAM). Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x34,period=200003,umask=0x20/
  mem_bound_stalls.ifetch_l2_hit
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to an instruction
        cache or TLB miss which hit in the L2 cache. Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x34,period=200003,umask=8/
  mem_bound_stalls.ifetch_llc_hit
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to an instruction
        cache or TLB miss which hit in the LLC or other core with HITE/F/M.
        Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x34,period=200003,umask=0x10/
  mem_bound_stalls.load
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to a demand load
        miss which hit in the L2,LLC,DRAM or MMIO (Non-DRAM). Unit: cpu_atom]
        cpu_atom/event=0x34,period=200003,umask=7/

        twice as noted below. Each count represents 8 computation operations,
        one for each element. Applies to SSE* and AVX* packed single precision
        floating-point instructions: ADD SUB HADD HSUB SUBADD MUL DIV MIN MAX
        SQRT RSQRT RCP DPP FM(N)ADD/SUB. DPP and FM(N)ADD/SUB instructions
        count twice as they perform 2 calculations per element. Unit: cpu_core]
        cpu_core/event=0xc7,period=100003,umask=0x20/
  fp_arith_inst_retired.4_flops
       [Number of SSE/AVX computational 128-bit packed single and 256-bit
        packed double precision FP instructions retired; some instructions
        will count twice as noted below. Each count represents 2 or/and 4
        computation operations,1 for each element. Applies to SSE* and AVX*
        packed single precision and packed double precision FP instructions:
        ADD SUB HADD HSUB SUBADD MUL DIV MIN MAX RCP14 RSQRT14 SQRT DPP
        FM(N)ADD/SUB. DPP and FM(N)ADD/SUB count twice as they perform 2
        calculations per element. Unit: cpu_core]
        cpu_core/event=0xc7,period=100003,umask=0x18/
  fp_arith_inst_retired.scalar
       [Number of SSE/AVX computational scalar floating-point instructions
        retired; some instructions will count twice as noted below. Applies to
        SSE* and AVX* scalar,double and single precision floating-point: ADD
        SUB MUL DIV MIN MAX RCP14 RSQRT14 RANGE SQRT DPP FM(N)ADD/SUB. DPP and
        FM(N)ADD/SUB instructions count twice as they perform multiple
        calculations per element. Unit: cpu_core]
        cpu_core/event=0xc7,period=1000003,umask=3/
  fp_arith_inst_retired.scalar_double
       [Counts number of SSE/AVX computational scalar double precision
        floating-point instructions retired; some instructions will count
        twice as noted below. Each count represents 1 computational operation.
        Applies to SSE* and AVX* scalar double precision floating-point
        instructions: ADD SUB MUL DIV MIN MAX SQRT FM(N)ADD/SUB. FM(N)ADD/SUB
        instructions count twice as they perform 2 calculations per element.
        Unit: cpu_core]
        cpu_core/event=0xc7,period=100003,umask=1/
  fp_arith_inst_retired.scalar_single
       [Counts number of SSE/AVX computational scalar single precision

software:
  alignment-faults
       [Number of kernel handled memory alignment faults. Unit: software]
        software/config=7/
  bpf-output
       [An event used by BPF programs to write to the perf ring buffer. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=0xa/
  cgroup-switches
       [Number of context switches to a task in a different cgroup. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=0xb/
  context-switches
       [Number of context switches [This event is an alias of cs]. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=3/
  cpu-clock
       [Per-CPU high-resolution timer based event. Unit: software]
        software/config=0/
  cpu-migrations
       [Number of times a process has migrated to a new CPU [This event is an
        alias of migrations]. Unit: software]
        software/config=4/
  cs
       [Number of context switches [This event is an alias of
        context-switches]. Unit: software]
        software/config=3/
  dummy
       [A placeholder event that doesn't count anything. Unit: software]
        software/config=9/
  emulation-faults
       [Number of kernel handled unimplemented instruction faults handled
        through emulation. Unit: software]
        software/config=8/
  faults
       [Number of page faults [This event is an alias of page-faults]. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=2/
  major-faults
       [Number of major page faults. Major faults require I/O to handle. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=6/
  migrations
       [Number of times a process has migrated to a new CPU [This event is an
        alias of cpu-migrations]. Unit: software]
        software/config=4/
  minor-faults
       [Number of minor page faults. Minor faults don't require I/O to handle.
        Unit: software]
        software/config=5/
  page-faults
       [Number of page faults [This event is an alias of faults]. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=2/
  task-clock
       [Per-task high-resolution timer based event. Unit: software]
        software/config=1/

tool:
  core_wide
       [1 if not SMT,if SMT are events being gathered on all SMT threads 1
        otherwise 0. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=0xd/
  duration_time
       [Wall clock interval time in nanoseconds. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=1/
  has_pmem
       [1 if persistent memory installed otherwise 0. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=4/
  num_cores
       [Number of cores. A core consists of 1 or more thread,with each thread
        being associated with a logical Linux CPU. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=5/
  num_cpus
       [Number of logical Linux CPUs. There may be multiple such CPUs on a
        core. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=6/
  num_cpus_online
       [Number of online logical Linux CPUs. There may be multiple such CPUs
        on a core. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=7/
  num_dies
       [Number of dies. Each die has 1 or more cores. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=8/
  num_packages
       [Number of packages. Each package has 1 or more die. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=9/
  smt_on
       [1 if simultaneous multithreading (aka hyperthreading) is enable
        otherwise 0. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=0xb/
  system_time
       [System/kernel time in nanoseconds. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=3/
  system_tsc_freq
       [The amount a Time Stamp Counter (TSC) increases per second. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=0xc/
  target_cpu
       [1 if CPUs being analyzed,0 if threads/processes. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=0xe/
  user_time
       [User (non-kernel) time in nanoseconds. Unit: tool]
        tool/config=2/

  unc_arb_trk_occupancy.all

Metric Groups:

Backend: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [max(0,tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound)]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_info_core_ilp
       [Instruction-Level-Parallelism (average number of uops executed when
        there is execution) per thread (logical-processor)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@ / cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD\,
        cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki
       [L2 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L2_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_memory_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the Memory subsystem within
        the Backend was a bottleneck]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-mem\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]

Bad:
  tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead
       [Total pipeline cost of irregular execution (e.g]
       [100 * ((1 - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ /
        cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency *
        (tma_ms_switches + tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers +
        tma_mispredicts_resteers * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) / (tma_clears_resteers +
        tma_mispredicts_resteers + tma_unknown_branches)) /
        (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) + tma_ms) + 10 *
        tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts * tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_machine_clears *
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes + tma_core_bound *
        (tma_serializing_operation + cpu_core@RS.EMPTY_RESOURCE@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks * tma_ports_utilized_0) / (tma_divider +
        tma_ports_utilization + tma_serializing_operation) +
        tma_microcode_sequencer / (tma_few_uops_instructions +
        tma_microcode_sequencer) * (tma_assists / tma_microcode_sequencer) *
        tma_heavy_operations)]
       [tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead > 10]
  tma_bottleneck_mispredictions
       [Total pipeline cost of Branch Misprediction related bottlenecks]
       [100 * (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) * (tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_fetch_latency
        * tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_bottleneck_mispredictions > 20]
  tma_info_bad_spec_branch_misprediction_cost
       [Branch Misprediction Cost: Cycles representing fraction of TMA slots
        wasted per non-speculative branch misprediction (retired JEClear)]
       [tma_bottleneck_mispredictions * tma_info_thread_slots / 6 /
        cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ / 100]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_ntaken
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for conditional non-taken
        branches (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_ntaken < 200]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_taken
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for conditional taken branches
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_taken < 200]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for indirect CALL or JMP branches
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.INDIRECT@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect < 1e3]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_ret
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for return branches (lower number
        means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.RET@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_ret < 500]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict
       [Number of Instructions per non-speculative Branch Misprediction
        (JEClear) (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict < 200]
  tma_info_branches_callret
       [Fraction of branches that are CALL or RET]
       [(cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@ +
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_RETURN@) /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_cond_nt
       [Fraction of branches that are non-taken conditionals]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_cond_tk
       [Fraction of branches that are taken conditionals]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_jump
       [Fraction of branches that are unconditional (direct or indirect) jumps]
       [(cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@ -
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@ - 2 *
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@) /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_other_branches
       [Fraction of branches of other types (not individually covered by other
        metrics in Info.Branches group)]
       [1 - (tma_info_branches_cond_nt + tma_info_branches_cond_tk +
        tma_info_branches_callret + tma_info_branches_jump)]

BadSpec: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_mispredictions
       [Total pipeline cost of Branch Misprediction related bottlenecks]
       [100 * (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) * (tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_fetch_latency
        * tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_bottleneck_mispredictions > 20]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-br\-mispredict@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_clears_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Machine Clears]
       [(1 - tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation) *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_clears_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict
       [Number of Instructions per non-speculative Branch Misprediction
        (JEClear) (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict < 200]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [max(0,tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts)]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_mispredicts_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Branch Misprediction at execution stage]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mispredicts_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

BigFootprint: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_big_code
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch related bottlenecks by large
        code footprint programs (i-side cache; TLB and BTB misses)]
       [100 * tma_fetch_latency * (tma_itlb_misses + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_unknown_branches) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches)]
       [tma_bottleneck_big_code > 20]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_TAG.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_unknown_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        new branch address clears]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_unknown_branches > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

BrMispredicts: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_mispredictions
       [Total pipeline cost of Branch Misprediction related bottlenecks]
       [100 * (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) * (tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_fetch_latency
        * tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_bottleneck_mispredictions > 20]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-br\-mispredict@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_info_bad_spec_branch_misprediction_cost
       [Branch Misprediction Cost: Cycles representing fraction of TMA slots
        wasted per non-speculative branch misprediction (retired JEClear)]
       [tma_bottleneck_mispredictions * tma_info_thread_slots / 6 /
        cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ / 100]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_ntaken
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for conditional non-taken
        branches (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_ntaken < 200]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_taken
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for conditional taken branches
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_cond_taken < 200]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for indirect CALL or JMP branches
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.INDIRECT@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect < 1e3]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_ret
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for return branches (lower number
        means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.RET@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_ret < 500]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict
       [Number of Instructions per non-speculative Branch Misprediction
        (JEClear) (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict < 200]
  tma_info_bad_spec_spec_clears_ratio
       [Speculative to Retired ratio of all clears (covering Mispredicts and
        nukes)]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEARS_COUNT@ /
        (cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ +
        cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@)]
  tma_mispredicts_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Branch Misprediction at execution stage]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mispredicts_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_other_mispredicts
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        other cases of misprediction (non-retired x86 branches or other types)]
       [max(tma_branch_mispredicts * (1 -
        cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ /
        (cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEARS_COUNT@ - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@)),
        0.0001)]
       [tma_other_mispredicts > 0.05 & (tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]

Branches: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_fused_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        fused instructions -- where one uop can represent multiple contiguous
        instructions]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.MACRO_FUSED@ /
        (tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fused_instructions > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_info_branches_callret
       [Fraction of branches that are CALL or RET]
       [(cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@ +
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_RETURN@) /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_cond_nt
       [Fraction of branches that are non-taken conditionals]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_cond_tk
       [Fraction of branches that are taken conditionals]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_jump
       [Fraction of branches that are unconditional (direct or indirect) jumps]
       [(cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@ -
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@ - 2 *
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@) /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_other_branches
       [Fraction of branches of other types (not individually covered by other
        metrics in Info.Branches group)]
       [1 - (tma_info_branches_cond_nt + tma_info_branches_cond_tk +
        tma_info_branches_callret + tma_info_branches_jump)]
  tma_info_frontend_tbpc
       [Taken Branches retired Per Cycle]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
  tma_info_inst_mix_bptkbranch
       [Branch instructions per taken branch]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch
       [Instructions per Branch (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch < 8]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall
       [Instructions per (near) call (lower number means higher occurrence
        rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall < 200]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 13]
  tma_info_system_ipfarbranch
       [Instructions per Far Branch ( Far Branches apply upon transition from
        application to operating system,handling interrupts,exceptions) [lower
        number means higher occurrence rate]]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH@u]
       [tma_info_system_ipfarbranch < 1e6]
  tma_info_thread_uptb
       [Uops per taken branch]
       [tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_thread_uptb < 9]
  tma_non_fused_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        branch instructions that were not fused]
       [tma_light_operations * (cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ -
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.MACRO_FUSED@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_non_fused_branches > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]

BvBC: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_big_code
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch related bottlenecks by large
        code footprint programs (i-side cache; TLB and BTB misses)]
       [100 * tma_fetch_latency * (tma_itlb_misses + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_unknown_branches) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches)]
       [tma_bottleneck_big_code > 20]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_TAG.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_unknown_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        new branch address clears]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_unknown_branches > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

BvBO: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_branching_overhead
       [Total pipeline cost of instructions used for program control-flow - a
        subset of the Retiring category in TMA]
       [100 * ((cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ + 2 *
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@ + cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.NOP@) /
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_bottleneck_branching_overhead > 5]
  tma_fused_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        fused instructions -- where one uop can represent multiple contiguous
        instructions]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.MACRO_FUSED@ /
        (tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fused_instructions > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_non_fused_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        branch instructions that were not fused]
       [tma_light_operations * (cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ -
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.MACRO_FUSED@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_non_fused_branches > 0.1 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_nop_instructions
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        NOP (no op) instructions]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.NOP@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_nop_instructions > 0.1 & (tma_other_light_ops > 0.3 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]

BvCB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est
       [Total pipeline cost when the execution is compute-bound - an
        estimation]
       [100 * (tma_core_bound * tma_divider / (tma_divider +
        tma_ports_utilization + tma_serializing_operation) + tma_core_bound *
        (tma_ports_utilization / (tma_divider + tma_ports_utilization +
        tma_serializing_operation)) * (tma_ports_utilized_3m /
        (tma_ports_utilized_0 + tma_ports_utilized_1 + tma_ports_utilized_2 +
        tma_ports_utilized_3m)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est > 20]
  tma_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Divider unit was
        active]
       [cpu_core@ARITH.DIV_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_ports_utilized_3m
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 3 or
        more uops per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.CYCLES_GE_3@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_3m > 0.4 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvFB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch bandwidth related bottlenecks
        (when the front-end could not sustain operations delivery to the
        back-end)]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound - (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer *
        tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) * tma_fetch_latency *
        tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) - (1
        - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ / cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,
        cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency * (tma_ms_switches +
        tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers
        * tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) /
        (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers +
        tma_unknown_branches)) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) +
        tma_ms)) - tma_bottleneck_big_code]
       [tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw > 20]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) - cpu_core@INT_MISC.UOP_DROPPING@ /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]

BvIO: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops delivered
        by the Microcode_Sequencer as a result of Assists]
       [78 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.ANY@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_assists > 0.1 & (tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 &
        tma_heavy_operations > 0.1)]
  tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead
       [Total pipeline cost of irregular execution (e.g]
       [100 * ((1 - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ /
        cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency *
        (tma_ms_switches + tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers +
        tma_mispredicts_resteers * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) / (tma_clears_resteers +
        tma_mispredicts_resteers + tma_unknown_branches)) /
        (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) + tma_ms) + 10 *
        tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts * tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_machine_clears *
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes + tma_core_bound *
        (tma_serializing_operation + cpu_core@RS.EMPTY_RESOURCE@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks * tma_ports_utilized_0) / (tma_divider +
        tma_ports_utilization + tma_serializing_operation) +
        tma_microcode_sequencer / (tma_few_uops_instructions +
        tma_microcode_sequencer) * (tma_assists / tma_microcode_sequencer) *
        tma_heavy_operations)]
       [tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead > 10]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) - cpu_core@INT_MISC.UOP_DROPPING@ /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_other_mispredicts
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        other cases of misprediction (non-retired x86 branches or other types)]
       [max(tma_branch_mispredicts * (1 -
        cpu_core@BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ /
        (cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEARS_COUNT@ - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@)),
        0.0001)]
       [tma_other_mispredicts > 0.05 & (tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]
  tma_other_nukes
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Nukes (Machine Clears) not related to memory ordering]
       [max(tma_machine_clears * (1 - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.MEMORY_ORDERING@
        / cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@),0.0001)]
       [tma_other_nukes > 0.05 & (tma_machine_clears > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]
  tma_serializing_operation
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU issue-pipeline was
        stalled due to serializing operations]
       [cpu_core@RESOURCE_STALLS.SCOREBOARD@ / tma_info_thread_clks +
        tma_c02_wait]
       [tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]

BvMB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Bandwidth related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_bandwidth / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_sq_full /
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_fb_full / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth > 20]
  tma_fb_full
       [This metric does a *rough estimation* of how often L1D Fill Buffer
        unavailability limited additional L1D miss memory access requests to
        proceed]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.FB_FULL@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fb_full > 0.3]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=4@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(cpu_core@XQ.FULL_CYCLES@ + cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.L2_STALLS@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvML: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Latency related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_latency / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_l3_hit_latency
        / (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * tma_l2_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_l1_latency_dependency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_lock_latency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_loads / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_stores / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_store_latency / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency > 20]
  tma_l1_latency_dependency
       [This metric ([SKL+] roughly; [LNL]) estimates fraction of cycles with
        demand load accesses that hit the L1D cache]
       [min(2 * (cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ -
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@) * 20 / 100,
        max(cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_MEM_ANY@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_L1D_MISS@,0)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l1_latency_dependency > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_hit_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L3 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L3 latency
        limited)]
       [9 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L3_HIT@ * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_hit_latency > 0.1 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(cpu_core@MEM_STORE_RETIRED.L2_HIT@ * 10 * (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) + (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) *
        min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvMP: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_mispredictions
       [Total pipeline cost of Branch Misprediction related bottlenecks]
       [100 * (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) * (tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_fetch_latency
        * tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_bottleneck_mispredictions > 20]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-br\-mispredict@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_mispredicts_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Branch Misprediction at execution stage]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mispredicts_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

BvMS: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Synchronization related bottlenecks
        (data transfers and coherency updates across processors)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) *
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing) / (tma_contested_accesses
        + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency + tma_sq_full) +
        tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) * tma_false_sharing / (tma_dtlb_store
        + tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores - tma_store_latency)) + tma_machine_clears * (1 -
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization > 10]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(25 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ *
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) + 24 *
        tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS@) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [24 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_NO_FWD@ +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ * (1 -
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [28 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [max(0,tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts)]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

BvMT: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Address Translation related bottlenecks
        (data-side TLBs)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / max(tma_memory_bound,
        tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_load / max(tma_l1_bound,tma_dtlb_load +
        tma_fb_full + tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency +
        tma_split_loads + tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound *
        (tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_store / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs > 20]
  tma_dtlb_load
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the Data
        TLB (DTLB) was missed by load accesses]
       [min(7 * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.STLB_HIT\,cmask\=1@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@,
        max(cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_MEM_ANY@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_L1D_MISS@,0)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_store
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles spent handling
        first-level data TLB store misses]
       [(7 * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.STLB_HIT\,cmask\=1@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvOB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_backend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where no uops are being
        delivered due to a lack of required resources for accepting new uops
        in the Backend]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_bottleneck_other_bottlenecks
       [Total pipeline cost of remaining bottlenecks in the back-end]
       [100 - (tma_bottleneck_big_code + tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw +
        tma_bottleneck_mispredictions +
        tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth +
        tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency +
        tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs +
        tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization +
        tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est + tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead +
        tma_bottleneck_branching_overhead + tma_bottleneck_useful_work)]
       [tma_bottleneck_other_bottlenecks > 20]

BvUW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_useful_work
       [Total pipeline cost of "useful operations" - the portion of Retiring
        category not covered by Branching_Overhead nor Irregular_Overhead]
       [100 * (tma_retiring - (cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ + 2 *
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@ + cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.NOP@) /
        tma_info_thread_slots - tma_microcode_sequencer /
        (tma_few_uops_instructions + tma_microcode_sequencer) * (tma_assists /
        tma_microcode_sequencer) * tma_heavy_operations)]
       [tma_bottleneck_useful_work > 20]
  tma_retiring
       [This category represents fraction of slots utilized by useful work
        i.e. issued uops that eventually get retired]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_retiring > 0.7 | tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]

C0Wait: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_c01_wait
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due
        staying in C0.1 power-performance optimized state (Faster wakeup time;
        Smaller power savings)]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.C01@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_c01_wait > 0.05 & (tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_c02_wait
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due
        staying in C0.2 power-performance optimized state (Slower wakeup time;
        Larger power savings)]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.C02@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_c02_wait > 0.05 & (tma_serializing_operation > 0.1 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_system_c0_wait
       [Fraction of cycles the processor is waiting yet unhalted; covering
        legacy PAUSE instruction,as well as C0.1 / C0.2 power-performance
        optimized states]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.C0_WAIT@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_info_system_c0_wait > 0.05]

CacheHits: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_fb_hpki
       [Fill Buffer (FB) hits per kilo instructions for retired demand loads
        (L1D misses that merge into ongoing miss-handling entries)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l1mpki
       [L1 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l1mpki_load
       [L1 cache true misses per kilo instruction for all demand loads
        (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.ALL_DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2hpki_all
       [L2 cache hits per kilo instruction for all request types (including
        speculative)]
       [1e3 * (cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.REFERENCES@ - cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.MISS@) /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2hpki_load
       [L2 cache hits per kilo instruction for all demand loads (including
        speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_HIT@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki
       [L2 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L2_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_all
       [L2 cache ([RKL+] true) misses per kilo instruction for all request
        types (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_load
       [L2 cache ([RKL+] true) misses per kilo instruction for all demand
        loads (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L3_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

CacheMisses: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_rfo
       [Offcore requests (L2 cache miss) per kilo instruction for demand RFOs]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]

CodeGen: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_branches_cond_nt
       [Fraction of branches that are non-taken conditionals]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
  tma_info_branches_cond_tk
       [Fraction of branches that are taken conditionals]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_TAKEN@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]

Compute: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [max(0,tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound)]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_fp_scalar
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) scalar uops
        fraction the CPU has retired]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_scalar > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_fp_vector
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) vector uops
        fraction the CPU has retired aggregated across all vector widths]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.VECTOR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_fp_vector_128b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 128-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_fp_vector_256b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 256-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_int_vector_128b
       [This metric represents 128-bit vector Integer ADD/SUB/SAD or VNNI
        (Vector Neural Network Instructions) uops fraction the CPU has retired]
       [(cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.ADD_128@ +
        cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.VNNI_128@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_int_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_int_operations > 0.1 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_int_vector_256b
       [This metric represents 256-bit vector Integer ADD/SUB/SAD/MUL or VNNI
        (Vector Neural Network Instructions) uops fraction the CPU has retired]
       [(cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.ADD_256@ + cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.MUL_256@
        + cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.VNNI_256@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_int_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_int_operations > 0.1 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_port_0
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 0 ([SNB+] ALU; [HSW+] ALU and 2nd branch)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_0 > 0.6]
  tma_x87_use
       [This metric serves as an approximation of legacy x87 usage]
       [tma_retiring * cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.X87@ /
        cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@]
       [tma_x87_use > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations > 0.6)]

Cor: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est
       [Total pipeline cost when the execution is compute-bound - an
        estimation]
       [100 * (tma_core_bound * tma_divider / (tma_divider +
        tma_ports_utilization + tma_serializing_operation) + tma_core_bound *
        (tma_ports_utilization / (tma_divider + tma_ports_utilization +
        tma_serializing_operation)) * (tma_ports_utilized_3m /
        (tma_ports_utilized_0 + tma_ports_utilized_1 + tma_ports_utilized_2 +
        tma_ports_utilized_3m)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead
       [Total pipeline cost of irregular execution (e.g]
       [100 * ((1 - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ /
        cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency *
        (tma_ms_switches + tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers +
        tma_mispredicts_resteers * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts) / (tma_clears_resteers +
        tma_mispredicts_resteers + tma_unknown_branches)) /
        (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) + tma_ms) + 10 *
        tma_microcode_sequencer * tma_other_mispredicts /
        tma_branch_mispredicts * tma_branch_mispredicts + tma_machine_clears *
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes + tma_core_bound *
        (tma_serializing_operation + cpu_core@RS.EMPTY_RESOURCE@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks * tma_ports_utilized_0) / (tma_divider +
        tma_ports_utilization + tma_serializing_operation) +
        tma_microcode_sequencer / (tma_few_uops_instructions +
        tma_microcode_sequencer) * (tma_assists / tma_microcode_sequencer) *
        tma_heavy_operations)]
       [tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead > 10]
  tma_bottleneck_other_bottlenecks
       [Total pipeline cost of remaining bottlenecks in the back-end]
       [100 - (tma_bottleneck_big_code + tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw +
        tma_bottleneck_mispredictions +
        tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth +
        tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency +
        tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs +
        tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization +
        tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est + tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead +
        tma_bottleneck_branching_overhead + tma_bottleneck_useful_work)]
       [tma_bottleneck_other_bottlenecks > 20]
  tma_info_botlnk_l0_core_bound_likely
       [Probability of Core Bound bottleneck hidden by SMT-profiling artifacts]
       [(100 * (1 - tma_core_bound / tma_ports_utilization if tma_core_bound <
        tma_ports_utilization else 1) if tma_info_system_smt_2t_utilization >
        0.5 else 0)]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l0_core_bound_likely > 0.5]
  tma_info_core_fp_arith_utilization
       [Actual per-core usage of the Floating Point non-X87 execution units
        (regardless of precision or vector-width)]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_1@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_5@) / (2 * tma_info_core_core_clks)]
  tma_info_core_ilp
       [Instruction-Level-Parallelism (average number of uops executed when
        there is execution) per thread (logical-processor)]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@ / cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD\,
        cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_pipeline_execute
       [Mem;Backend;CacheHits]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE_CYCLES_GE_1@ / 2 if #SMT_on else
        cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD\,cmask\=1@)]
  tma_info_system_gflops
       [Giga Floating Point Operations Per Second]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ + 2 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ + 4 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.4_FLOPS@ + 8 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_thread_execute_per_issue
       [The ratio of Executed- by Issued-Uops]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_EXECUTED.THREAD@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@]

DSB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of DSB (uop cache) hits - subset of the
        Instruction_Fetch_BW Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound * (tma_fetch_bandwidth /
        (tma_fetch_bandwidth + tma_fetch_latency)) * (tma_dsb / (tma_dsb +
        tma_lsd + tma_mite + tma_ms)))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_bandwidth > 10]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the DSB (aka Decoded ICache; or Uop
        Cache)]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_UOPS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@]
       [tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage < 0.7 & tma_info_thread_ipc / 6 > 0.35]

DSBmiss: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_decoder0_alone
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where decoder-0 was the only
        active decoder]
       [(cpu_core@INST_DECODED.DECODERS\,cmask\=1@ -
        cpu_core@INST_DECODED.DECODERS\,cmask\=2@) / tma_info_core_core_clks /
        2]
       [tma_decoder0_alone > 0.1 & (tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth >
        0.2)]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [cpu_core@DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_misses
       [Total pipeline cost of DSB (uop cache) misses - subset of the
        Instruction_Fetch_BW Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_fetch_latency * tma_dsb_switches / (tma_branch_resteers +
        tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp +
        tma_ms_switches) + tma_fetch_bandwidth * tma_mite / (tma_dsb + tma_lsd
        + tma_mite + tma_ms))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_misses > 10]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_switch_cost
       [Average number of cycles of a switch from the DSB fetch-unit to MITE
        fetch unit - see DSB_Switches tree node for details]
       [cpu_core@DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES@ /
        cpu_core@DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES\,cmask\=1\,edge@]
  tma_info_frontend_ipdsb_miss_ret
       [Instructions per non-speculative DSB miss (lower number means higher
        occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@FRONTEND_RETIRED.ANY_DSB_MISS@]
       [tma_info_frontend_ipdsb_miss_ret < 50]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]

DataSharing: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(25 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ *
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) + 24 *
        tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS@) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [28 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

Default:
  CPUs_utilized
       [Average CPU utilization]
       [(software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@) / (duration_time * 1e9)]
  backend_cycles_idle
       [Backend stalls per cycle]
       [(stalled\-cycles\-backend / cpu\-cycles if
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-backend) else 0)]
       [backend_cycles_idle > 0.2]
  branch_frequency
       [Branches per CPU second]
       [branches / (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  branch_miss_rate
       [Branch miss rate]
       [branch\-misses / branches]
       [branch_miss_rate > 0.05]
  cs_per_second
       [Context switches per CPU second]
       [software@context\-switches\,name\=context\-switches@ * 1e9 /
        (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  cycles_frequency
       [Cycles per CPU second]
       [cpu\-cycles / (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu
        else software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  frontend_cycles_idle
       [Frontend stalls per cycle]
       [(stalled\-cycles\-frontend / cpu\-cycles if
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-frontend) else 0)]
       [frontend_cycles_idle > 0.1]
  insn_per_cycle
       [Instructions Per Cycle]
       [instructions / cpu\-cycles]
       [insn_per_cycle < 1]
  migrations_per_second
       [Process migrations to a new CPU per CPU second]
       [software@cpu\-migrations\,name\=cpu\-migrations@ * 1e9 /
        (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  page_faults_per_second
       [Page faults per CPU second]
       [software@page\-faults\,name\=page\-faults@ * 1e9 /
        (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  stalled_cycles_per_instruction
       [Max front or backend stalls per instruction]
       [(max(stalled\-cycles\-frontend,stalled\-cycles\-backend) /
        instructions if has_event(stalled\-cycles\-frontend) &
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-backend) else (stalled\-cycles\-frontend /
        instructions if has_event(stalled\-cycles\-frontend) else
        (stalled\-cycles\-backend / instructions if
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-backend) else 0)))]
  tma_backend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where no uops are being
        delivered due to a lack of required resources for accepting new uops
        in the Backend]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_bad_speculation
       [This category represents fraction of slots wasted due to incorrect
        speculations]
       [max(1 - (tma_frontend_bound + tma_backend_bound + tma_retiring),0)]
       [tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) - cpu_core@INT_MISC.UOP_DROPPING@ /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_retiring
       [This category represents fraction of slots utilized by useful work
        i.e. issued uops that eventually get retired]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@)]
       [tma_retiring > 0.7 | tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]

Default2:
  l1d_miss_rate
       [L1D miss rate]
       [L1\-dcache\-load\-misses / L1\-dcache\-loads]
       [l1d_miss_rate > 0.05]
  llc_miss_rate
       [LLC miss rate]
       [LLC\-load\-misses / LLC\-loads]
       [llc_miss_rate > 0.05]

Default3:
  dtlb_miss_rate
       [dTLB miss rate]
       [dTLB\-load\-misses / dTLB\-loads]
       [dtlb_miss_rate > 0.05]
  itlb_miss_rate
       [iTLB miss rate]
       [iTLB\-load\-misses / iTLB\-loads]
       [itlb_miss_rate > 0.05]
  l1i_miss_rate
       [L1I miss rate]
       [L1\-icache\-load\-misses / L1\-icache\-loads]
       [l1i_miss_rate > 0.05]

Default4:
  l1_prefetch_miss_rate
       [L1 prefetch miss rate]
       [L1\-dcache\-prefetch\-misses / L1\-dcache\-prefetches]
       [l1_prefetch_miss_rate > 0.05]

Fed: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_big_code
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch related bottlenecks by large
        code footprint programs (i-side cache; TLB and BTB misses)]
       [100 * tma_fetch_latency * (tma_itlb_misses + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_unknown_branches) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches)]
       [tma_bottleneck_big_code > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch bandwidth related bottlenecks
        (when the front-end could not sustain operations delivery to the
        back-end)]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound - (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer *
        tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) * tma_fetch_latency *
        tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) - (1
        - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ / cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,
        cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency * (tma_ms_switches +
        tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers
        * tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) /
        (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers +
        tma_unknown_branches)) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) +
        tma_ms)) - tma_bottleneck_big_code]
       [tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw > 20]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of DSB (uop cache) hits - subset of the
        Instruction_Fetch_BW Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound * (tma_fetch_bandwidth /
        (tma_fetch_bandwidth + tma_fetch_latency)) * (tma_dsb / (tma_dsb +
        tma_lsd + tma_mite + tma_ms)))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_bandwidth > 10]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_misses
       [Total pipeline cost of DSB (uop cache) misses - subset of the
        Instruction_Fetch_BW Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_fetch_latency * tma_dsb_switches / (tma_branch_resteers +
        tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp +
        tma_ms_switches) + tma_fetch_bandwidth * tma_mite / (tma_dsb + tma_lsd
        + tma_mite + tma_ms))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_misses > 10]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_ic_misses
       [Total pipeline cost of Instruction Cache misses - subset of the
        Big_Code Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_fetch_latency * tma_icache_misses / (tma_branch_resteers +
        tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp +
        tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_ic_misses > 5]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the DSB (aka Decoded ICache; or Uop
        Cache)]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_UOPS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@]
       [tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage < 0.7 & tma_info_thread_ipc / 6 > 0.35]
  tma_info_frontend_fetch_upc
       [Average number of Uops issued by front-end when it issued something]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY\,cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_frontend_icache_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L1 instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS\,cmask\=1\,
        edge@]
  tma_info_frontend_ipdsb_miss_ret
       [Instructions per non-speculative DSB miss (lower number means higher
        occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@FRONTEND_RETIRED.ANY_DSB_MISS@]
       [tma_info_frontend_ipdsb_miss_ret < 50]
  tma_info_frontend_ipunknown_branch
       [Instructions per speculative Unknown Branch Misprediction (BAClear)
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / cpu_core@BACLEARS.ANY@]
  tma_info_frontend_lsd_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the LSD (Loop Stream Detector; aka Loop
        Cache)]
       [cpu_core@LSD.UOPS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@]
  tma_info_frontend_unknown_branch_cost
       [Average number of cycles the front-end was delayed due to an Unknown
        Branch detection]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES@ /
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES\,cmask\=1\,edge@]
  tma_info_inst_mix_bptkbranch
       [Branch instructions per taken branch]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@ /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch
       [Instructions per Branch (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch < 8]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall
       [Instructions per (near) call (lower number means higher occurrence
        rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall < 200]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 13]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_code_stlb_mpki
       [STLB (2nd level TLB) code speculative misses per kilo instruction
        (misses of any page-size that complete the page walk)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_pipeline_fetch_dsb
       [Average number of uops fetched from DSB per cycle]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_UOPS@ / cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_ANY@]
  tma_info_pipeline_fetch_lsd
       [Average number of uops fetched from LSD per cycle]
       [cpu_core@LSD.UOPS@ / cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_ACTIVE@]
  tma_info_pipeline_fetch_mite
       [Average number of uops fetched from MITE per cycle]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_UOPS@ / cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_ANY@]
  tma_info_thread_uptb
       [Uops per taken branch]
       [tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_thread_uptb < 9]

FetchBW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch bandwidth related bottlenecks
        (when the front-end could not sustain operations delivery to the
        back-end)]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound - (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer *
        tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) * tma_fetch_latency *
        tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) - (1
        - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ / cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,
        cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency * (tma_ms_switches +
        tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers
        * tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) /
        (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers +
        tma_unknown_branches)) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) +
        tma_ms)) - tma_bottleneck_big_code]
       [tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw > 20]
  tma_decoder0_alone
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where decoder-0 was the only
        active decoder]
       [(cpu_core@INST_DECODED.DECODERS\,cmask\=1@ -
        cpu_core@INST_DECODED.DECODERS\,cmask\=2@) / tma_info_core_core_clks /
        2]
       [tma_decoder0_alone > 0.1 & (tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth >
        0.2)]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [max(0,tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency)]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of DSB (uop cache) hits - subset of the
        Instruction_Fetch_BW Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound * (tma_fetch_bandwidth /
        (tma_fetch_bandwidth + tma_fetch_latency)) * (tma_dsb / (tma_dsb +
        tma_lsd + tma_mite + tma_ms)))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_dsb_bandwidth > 10]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the DSB (aka Decoded ICache; or Uop
        Cache)]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_UOPS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@]
       [tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage < 0.7 & tma_info_thread_ipc / 6 > 0.35]
  tma_info_frontend_fetch_upc
       [Average number of Uops issued by front-end when it issued something]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY\,cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_frontend_tbpc
       [Taken Branches retired Per Cycle]
       [cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 13]
  tma_info_pipeline_fetch_dsb
       [Average number of uops fetched from DSB per cycle]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_UOPS@ / cpu_core@IDQ.DSB_CYCLES_ANY@]
  tma_info_pipeline_fetch_lsd
