       [Average number of uops fetched from LSD per cycle]
       [cpu_core@LSD.UOPS@ / cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_ACTIVE@]
  tma_info_pipeline_fetch_mite
       [Average number of uops fetched from MITE per cycle]
       [cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_UOPS@ / cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_ANY@]
  tma_info_thread_uptb
       [Uops per taken branch]
       [tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots /
        cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_thread_uptb < 9]
  tma_lsd
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to LSD (Loop Stream Detector) unit]
       [(cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_ACTIVE@ - cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_lsd > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_ANY@ - cpu_core@IDQ.MITE_CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]

FetchLat: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_branch_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks +
        tma_unknown_branches]
       [tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_code_l2_hit
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that hit in the L2 cache]
       [max(0,tma_icache_misses - tma_code_l2_miss)]
       [tma_code_l2_hit > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_l2_miss
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that miss in the L2 cache]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_CODE_RD@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_l2_miss > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the (first
        level) ITLB was missed by instructions fetches,that later on hit in
        second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [max(0,tma_itlb_misses - tma_code_stlb_miss)]
       [tma_code_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the Second-level
        TLB (STLB) was missed by instruction fetches,performing a hardware
        page walk]
       [cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for (instruction)
        code accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for (instruction) code
        accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [cpu_core@DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_ic_misses
       [Total pipeline cost of Instruction Cache misses - subset of the
        Big_Code Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_fetch_latency * tma_icache_misses / (tma_branch_resteers +
        tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp +
        tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_ic_misses > 5]
  tma_info_frontend_icache_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L1 instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS\,cmask\=1\,
        edge@]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_TAG.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_lcp
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU was stalled due to
        Length Changing Prefixes (LCPs)]
       [cpu_core@DECODE.LCP@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lcp > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [3 * cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1\,edge@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_unknown_branches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        new branch address clears]
       [cpu_core@INT_MISC.UNKNOWN_BRANCH_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_unknown_branches > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]

Flops: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_fp_scalar
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) scalar uops
        fraction the CPU has retired]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_scalar > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_fp_vector
       [This metric approximates arithmetic floating-point (FP) vector uops
        fraction the CPU has retired aggregated across all vector widths]
       [cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.VECTOR@ / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations >
        0.6)]
  tma_fp_vector_128b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 128-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_fp_vector_256b
       [This metric approximates arithmetic FP vector uops fraction the CPU
        has retired for 256-bit wide vectors]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_fp_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_fp_vector > 0.1 & (tma_fp_arith > 0.2
        & tma_light_operations > 0.6))]
  tma_info_core_flopc
       [Floating Point Operations Per Cycle]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ + 2 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ + 4 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.4_FLOPS@ + 8 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks]
  tma_info_core_fp_arith_utilization
       [Actual per-core usage of the Floating Point non-X87 execution units
        (regardless of precision or vector-width)]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_1@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_5@) / (2 * tma_info_core_core_clks)]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic instruction (lower number means higher
        occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.VECTOR@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx128
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic AVX/SSE 128-bit instruction (lower
        number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx128 < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx256
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic AVX* 256-bit instruction (lower number
        means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx256 < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_dp
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic Scalar Double-Precision instruction
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_DOUBLE@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_dp < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_sp
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic Scalar Single-Precision instruction
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_SINGLE@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_sp < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipflop
       [Instructions per Floating Point (FP) Operation (lower number means
        higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ +
        2 * cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ + 4 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.4_FLOPS@ + 8 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipflop < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ippause
       [Instructions per PAUSE (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.PAUSE_INST@]
  tma_info_system_gflops
       [Giga Floating Point Operations Per Second]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ + 2 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ + 4 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.4_FLOPS@ + 8 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]

FpScalar: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_dp
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic Scalar Double-Precision instruction
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_DOUBLE@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_dp < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_sp
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic Scalar Single-Precision instruction
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_SINGLE@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_sp < 10]

FpVector: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx128
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic AVX/SSE 128-bit instruction (lower
        number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx128 < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx256
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic AVX* 256-bit instruction (lower number
        means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx256 < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ippause
       [Instructions per PAUSE (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.PAUSE_INST@]

Frontend: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_big_code
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch related bottlenecks by large
        code footprint programs (i-side cache; TLB and BTB misses)]
       [100 * tma_fetch_latency * (tma_itlb_misses + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_unknown_branches) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches)]
       [tma_bottleneck_big_code > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch bandwidth related bottlenecks
        (when the front-end could not sustain operations delivery to the
        back-end)]
       [100 * (tma_frontend_bound - (1 - 10 * tma_microcode_sequencer *
        tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) * tma_fetch_latency *
        tma_mispredicts_resteers / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) - (1
        - cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ / cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,
        cmask\=1@) * (tma_fetch_latency * (tma_ms_switches +
        tma_branch_resteers * (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers
        * tma_other_mispredicts / tma_branch_mispredicts) /
        (tma_clears_resteers + tma_mispredicts_resteers +
        tma_unknown_branches)) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches) +
        tma_ms)) - tma_bottleneck_big_code]
       [tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw > 20]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [max(0,tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency)]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend latency issues]
       [cpu_core@topdown\-fetch\-lat@ / (cpu_core@topdown\-fe\-bound@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-bad\-spec@ + cpu_core@topdown\-retiring@ +
        cpu_core@topdown\-be\-bound@) - cpu_core@INT_MISC.UOP_DROPPING@ /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 13]

HPC: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_avx_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops as a
        result of handing SSE to AVX* or AVX* to SSE transition Assists]
       [63 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.SSE_AVX_MIX@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_avx_assists > 0.1]
  tma_fp_arith
       [This metric represents overall arithmetic floating-point (FP)
        operations fraction the CPU has executed (retired)]
       [tma_x87_use + tma_fp_scalar + tma_fp_vector]
       [tma_fp_arith > 0.2 & tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_fp_assists
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops
        as a result of handing Floating Point (FP) Assists]
       [30 * cpu_core@ASSISTS.FP@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fp_assists > 0.1]
  tma_info_core_fp_arith_utilization
       [Actual per-core usage of the Floating Point non-X87 execution units
        (regardless of precision or vector-width)]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_0@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_1@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_DISPATCHED.PORT_5@) / (2 * tma_info_core_core_clks)]
  tma_info_system_cpu_utilization
       [Average CPU Utilization (percentage)]
       [tma_info_system_cpus_utilized / #num_cpus_online]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]
  tma_info_system_gflops
       [Giga Floating Point Operations Per Second]
       [(cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ + 2 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ + 4 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.4_FLOPS@ + 8 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@) / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_shuffles_256b
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        Shuffle operations of 256-bit vector size (FP or Integer)]
       [tma_light_operations * cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.SHUFFLES@ /
        (tma_retiring * tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_shuffles_256b > 0.1 & (tma_other_light_ops > 0.3 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]

IcMiss: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_big_code
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch related bottlenecks by large
        code footprint programs (i-side cache; TLB and BTB misses)]
       [100 * tma_fetch_latency * (tma_itlb_misses + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_unknown_branches) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches)]
       [tma_bottleneck_big_code > 20]
  tma_code_l2_hit
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that hit in the L2 cache]
       [max(0,tma_icache_misses - tma_code_l2_miss)]
       [tma_code_l2_hit > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_l2_miss
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that miss in the L2 cache]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_CODE_RD@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_l2_miss > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_info_botlnk_l2_ic_misses
       [Total pipeline cost of Instruction Cache misses - subset of the
        Big_Code Bottleneck]
       [100 * (tma_fetch_latency * tma_icache_misses / (tma_branch_resteers +
        tma_dsb_switches + tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp +
        tma_ms_switches))]
       [tma_info_botlnk_l2_ic_misses > 5]
  tma_info_frontend_icache_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L1 instruction cache misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS@ / cpu_core@ICACHE_DATA.STALLS\,cmask\=1\,
        edge@]
  tma_info_frontend_l2mpki_code
       [L2 cache true code cacheline misses per kilo instruction]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@FRONTEND_RETIRED.L2_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_frontend_l2mpki_code_all
       [L2 cache speculative code cacheline misses per kilo instruction]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]

Ifetch: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_bottleneck_%_ifetch_miss_bound_cycles
       [Percentage of time that allocation and retirement is stalled by the
        Frontend Cluster due to an Ifetch Miss,either Icache or ITLB Miss]
       [100 * cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.IFETCH@ /
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@]

InsType: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic instruction (lower number means higher
        occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.VECTOR@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx128
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic AVX/SSE 128-bit instruction (lower
        number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx128 < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx256
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic AVX* 256-bit instruction (lower number
        means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ +
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_avx256 < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_dp
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic Scalar Double-Precision instruction
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_DOUBLE@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_dp < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_sp
       [Instructions per FP Arithmetic Scalar Single-Precision instruction
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ /
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_SINGLE@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iparith_scalar_sp < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch
       [Instructions per Branch (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch < 8]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipflop
       [Instructions per Floating Point (FP) Operation (lower number means
        higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / (cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR@ +
        2 * cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE@ + 4 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.4_FLOPS@ + 8 *
        cpu_core@FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE@)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipflop < 10]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipload
       [Instructions per Load (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipload < 3]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ippause
       [Instructions per PAUSE (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.PAUSE_INST@]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipstore
       [Instructions per Store (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipstore < 8]

IntVector: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_int_vector_128b
       [This metric represents 128-bit vector Integer ADD/SUB/SAD or VNNI
        (Vector Neural Network Instructions) uops fraction the CPU has retired]
       [(cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.ADD_128@ +
        cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.VNNI_128@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_int_vector_128b > 0.1 & (tma_int_operations > 0.1 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]
  tma_int_vector_256b
       [This metric represents 256-bit vector Integer ADD/SUB/SAD/MUL or VNNI
        (Vector Neural Network Instructions) uops fraction the CPU has retired]
       [(cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.ADD_256@ + cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.MUL_256@
        + cpu_core@INT_VEC_RETIRED.VNNI_256@) / (tma_retiring *
        tma_info_thread_slots)]
       [tma_int_vector_256b > 0.1 & (tma_int_operations > 0.1 &
        tma_light_operations > 0.6)]

LSD: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_frontend_lsd_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the LSD (Loop Stream Detector; aka Loop
        Cache)]
       [cpu_core@LSD.UOPS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@]
  tma_lsd
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to LSD (Loop Stream Detector) unit]
       [(cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_ACTIVE@ - cpu_core@LSD.CYCLES_OK@) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_lsd > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]

Load_Store_Miss: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_bottleneck_%_load_miss_bound_cycles
       [Percentage of time that retirement is stalled due to an L1 miss]
       [100 * cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD@ /
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@]

LockCont: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Synchronization related bottlenecks
        (data transfers and coherency updates across processors)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) *
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing) / (tma_contested_accesses
        + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency + tma_sq_full) +
        tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) * tma_false_sharing / (tma_dtlb_store
        + tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores - tma_store_latency)) + tma_machine_clears * (1 -
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization > 10]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(25 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ *
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) + 24 *
        tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS@) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [28 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD@]
  tma_lock_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling cache
        misses due to lock operations]
       [(16 * max(0,cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.ALL_RFO@) + cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@ * (10 *
        cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT@ + min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@))) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lock_latency > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(cpu_core@MEM_STORE_RETIRED.L2_HIT@ * 10 * (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) + (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) *
        min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

MachineClears: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_clears_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers as a result of Machine Clears]
       [(1 - tma_branch_mispredicts / tma_bad_speculation) *
        cpu_core@INT_MISC.CLEAR_RESTEER_CYCLES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_clears_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [max(0,tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts)]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

Machine_Clears: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_other_nukes
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Nukes (Machine Clears) not related to memory ordering]
       [max(tma_machine_clears * (1 - cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.MEMORY_ORDERING@
        / cpu_core@MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT@),0.0001)]
       [tma_other_nukes > 0.05 & (tma_machine_clears > 0.1 &
        tma_bad_speculation > 0.15)]

Mem:
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Bandwidth related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_bandwidth / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_sq_full /
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_fb_full / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Latency related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_latency / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_l3_hit_latency
        / (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * tma_l2_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_l1_latency_dependency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_lock_latency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_loads / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_stores / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_store_latency / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Address Translation related bottlenecks
        (data-side TLBs)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / max(tma_memory_bound,
        tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_load / max(tma_l1_bound,tma_dtlb_load +
        tma_fb_full + tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency +
        tma_split_loads + tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound *
        (tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_store / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Synchronization related bottlenecks
        (data transfers and coherency updates across processors)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) *
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing) / (tma_contested_accesses
        + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency + tma_sq_full) +
        tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) * tma_false_sharing / (tma_dtlb_store
        + tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores - tma_store_latency)) + tma_machine_clears * (1 -
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization > 10]
  tma_info_memory_core_l1d_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l2_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_access_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data access bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_access_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_fb_hpki
       [Fill Buffer (FB) hits per kilo instructions for retired demand loads
        (L1D misses that merge into ongoing miss-handling entries)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@L1D.REPLACEMENT@ / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l1mpki
       [L1 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l1mpki_load
       [L1 cache true misses per kilo instruction for all demand loads
        (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.ALL_DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@L2_LINES_IN.ALL@ / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l2hpki_all
       [L2 cache hits per kilo instruction for all request types (including
        speculative)]
       [1e3 * (cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.REFERENCES@ - cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.MISS@) /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2hpki_load
       [L2 cache hits per kilo instruction for all demand loads (including
        speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_HIT@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki
       [L2 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L2_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_all
       [L2 cache ([RKL+] true) misses per kilo instruction for all request
        types (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_load
       [L2 cache ([RKL+] true) misses per kilo instruction for all demand
        loads (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_access_bw
       [Average per-thread data access bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.ALL_REQUESTS@ / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@LONGEST_LAT_CACHE.MISS@ / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l3mpki
       [L3 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L3_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency
       [Actual Average Latency for L1 data-cache miss demand load operations
        (in core cycles)]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_COMPLETED.L1_MISS_ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_mix_bus_lock_pki
       ["Bus lock" per kilo instruction]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@SQ_MISC.BUS_LOCK@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_mix_uc_load_pki
       [Un-cacheable retired load per kilo instruction]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_MISC_RETIRED.UC@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_mlp
       [Memory-Level-Parallelism (average number of L1 miss demand load when
        there is at least one such miss]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING@ /
        cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES@]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_load_stlb_mpki
       [STLB (2nd level TLB) data load speculative misses per kilo instruction
        (misses of any page-size that complete the page walk)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization
       [Utilization of the core's Page Walker(s) serving STLB misses triggered
        by instruction/Load/Store accesses]
       [(cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_PENDING@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_PENDING@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_PENDING@) / (4 *
        tma_info_core_core_clks)]
       [tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization > 0.5]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_store_stlb_mpki
       [STLB (2nd level TLB) data store speculative misses per kilo
        instruction (misses of any page-size that complete the page walk)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_system_mem_parallel_reads
       [Average number of parallel data read requests to external memory]
       [UNC_ARB_DAT_OCCUPANCY.RD / UNC_ARB_DAT_OCCUPANCY.RD@cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_system_mem_read_latency
       [Average latency of data read request to external memory (in
        nanoseconds)]
       [(UNC_ARB_TRK_OCCUPANCY.RD + UNC_ARB_DAT_OCCUPANCY.RD) /
        UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.RD]
  tma_info_thread_cpi
       [Cycles Per Instruction (per Logical Processor)]
       [1 / tma_info_thread_ipc]

MemOffcore: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]

Mem_Exec: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_bottleneck_%_mem_exec_bound_cycles
       [Percentage of time that retirement is stalled by the Memory Cluster
        due to a pipeline stall]
       [100 * cpu_atom@LD_HEAD.ANY_AT_RET@ / cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@]

MemoryBW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Bandwidth related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_bandwidth / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_sq_full /
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_fb_full / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth > 20]
  tma_fb_full
       [This metric does a *rough estimation* of how often L1D Fill Buffer
        unavailability limited additional L1D miss memory access requests to
        proceed]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.FB_FULL@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fb_full > 0.3]
  tma_info_memory_core_l1d_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l2_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_access_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data access bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_access_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@L1D.REPLACEMENT@ / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@L2_LINES_IN.ALL@ / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_access_bw
       [Average per-thread data access bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.ALL_REQUESTS@ / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@LONGEST_LAT_CACHE.MISS@ / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_mlp
       [Memory-Level-Parallelism (average number of L1 miss demand load when
        there is at least one such miss]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING@ /
        cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES@]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]
  tma_info_system_mem_parallel_reads
       [Average number of parallel data read requests to external memory]
       [UNC_ARB_DAT_OCCUPANCY.RD / UNC_ARB_DAT_OCCUPANCY.RD@cmask\=1@]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=4@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(cpu_core@XQ.FULL_CYCLES@ + cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.L2_STALLS@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_streaming_stores
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to Streaming store
        memory accesses; Streaming store optimize out a read request required
        by RFO stores]
       [9 * cpu_core@OCR.STREAMING_WR.ANY_RESPONSE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_streaming_stores > 0.2 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

MemoryBound: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dram_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled on accesses to
        external memory (DRAM) by loads]
       [cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L3_MISS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency
       [Actual Average Latency for L1 data-cache miss demand load operations
        (in core cycles)]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_COMPLETED.L1_MISS_ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_mlp
       [Memory-Level-Parallelism (average number of L1 miss demand load when
        there is at least one such miss]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING@ /
        cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES@]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [(cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_MISS@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L3_MISS@) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_store_bound
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to RFO store
        memory accesses; RFO store issue a read-for-ownership request before
        the write]
       [cpu_core@EXE_ACTIVITY.BOUND_ON_STORES@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

MemoryLat: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Latency related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_latency / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_l3_hit_latency
        / (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * tma_l2_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_l1_latency_dependency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_lock_latency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_loads / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_stores / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_store_latency / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency > 20]
  tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency
       [Actual Average Latency for L1 data-cache miss demand load operations
        (in core cycles)]
       [cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_COMPLETED.L1_MISS_ANY@]
  tma_info_system_mem_read_latency
       [Average latency of data read request to external memory (in
        nanoseconds)]
       [(UNC_ARB_TRK_OCCUPANCY.RD + UNC_ARB_DAT_OCCUPANCY.RD) /
        UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.RD]
  tma_l1_latency_dependency
       [This metric ([SKL+] roughly; [LNL]) estimates fraction of cycles with
        demand load accesses that hit the L1D cache]
       [min(2 * (cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ -
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@) * 20 / 100,
        max(cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_MEM_ANY@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_L1D_MISS@,0)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l1_latency_dependency > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_l2_hit_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L2 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L2 latency
        limited)]
       [3 * tma_info_system_core_frequency * cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L2_HIT@
        * (1 + cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@ / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_hit_latency > 0.05 & (tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_l3_hit_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L3 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L3 latency
        limited)]
       [9 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L3_HIT@ * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_hit_latency > 0.1 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(cpu_core@MEM_STORE_RETIRED.L2_HIT@ * 10 * (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) + (1 -
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@) *
        min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

MemoryTLB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_big_code
       [Total pipeline cost of instruction fetch related bottlenecks by large
        code footprint programs (i-side cache; TLB and BTB misses)]
       [100 * tma_fetch_latency * (tma_itlb_misses + tma_icache_misses +
        tma_unknown_branches) / (tma_branch_resteers + tma_dsb_switches +
        tma_icache_misses + tma_itlb_misses + tma_lcp + tma_ms_switches)]
       [tma_bottleneck_big_code > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Address Translation related bottlenecks
        (data-side TLBs)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / max(tma_memory_bound,
        tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_load / max(tma_l1_bound,tma_dtlb_load +
        tma_fb_full + tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency +
        tma_split_loads + tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound *
        (tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_store / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs > 20]
  tma_code_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the (first
        level) ITLB was missed by instructions fetches,that later on hit in
        second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [max(0,tma_itlb_misses - tma_code_stlb_miss)]
       [tma_code_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the Second-level
        TLB (STLB) was missed by instruction fetches,performing a hardware
        page walk]
       [cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for (instruction)
        code accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_code_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for (instruction) code
        accesses]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@)]
       [tma_code_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_code_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)))]
  tma_dtlb_load
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the Data
        TLB (DTLB) was missed by load accesses]
       [min(7 * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.STLB_HIT\,cmask\=1@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@,
        max(cpu_core@CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_MEM_ANY@ -
        cpu_core@MEMORY_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_L1D_MISS@,0)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_store
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles spent handling
        first-level data TLB store misses]
       [(7 * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.STLB_HIT\,cmask\=1@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_code_stlb_mpki
       [STLB (2nd level TLB) code speculative misses per kilo instruction
        (misses of any page-size that complete the page walk)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_load_stlb_mpki
       [STLB (2nd level TLB) data load speculative misses per kilo instruction
        (misses of any page-size that complete the page walk)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization
       [Utilization of the core's Page Walker(s) serving STLB misses triggered
        by instruction/Load/Store accesses]
       [(cpu_core@ITLB_MISSES.WALK_PENDING@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_PENDING@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_PENDING@) / (4 *
        tma_info_core_core_clks)]
       [tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization > 0.5]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_store_stlb_mpki
       [STLB (2nd level TLB) data store speculative misses per kilo
        instruction (misses of any page-size that complete the page walk)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED@ /
        cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [cpu_core@ICACHE_TAG.STALLS@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_load_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the (first
        level) DTLB was missed by load accesses,that later on hit in
        second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [tma_dtlb_load - tma_load_stlb_miss]
       [tma_load_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1
        & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the Second-level
        TLB (STLB) was missed by load accesses,performing a hardware page walk]
       [cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1
        & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss_1g
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 1 GB pages for data load accesses]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss_1g > 0.05 & (tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for data load
        accesses]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_load_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for data load accesses]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_load_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_load_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_store_stlb_hit
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the TLB was
        missed by store accesses,hitting in the second-level TLB (STLB)]
       [tma_dtlb_store - tma_store_stlb_miss]
       [tma_store_stlb_hit > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound
        > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles where the STLB was missed
        by store accesses,performing a hardware page walk]
       [cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_ACTIVE@ / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 & (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound
        > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss_1g
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 1 GB pages for data store accesses]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss_1g > 0.05 & (tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss_2m
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 2 or 4 MB pages for data store
        accesses]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@
        / (cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss_2m > 0.05 & (tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]
  tma_store_stlb_miss_4k
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles to walk the memory paging
        structures to cache translation of 4 KB pages for data store accesses]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss * cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ /
        (cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_4K@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_2M_4M@ +
        cpu_core@DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_COMPLETED_1G@)]
       [tma_store_stlb_miss_4k > 0.05 & (tma_store_stlb_miss > 0.05 &
        (tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))))]

Memory_BW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_latency_data_l2_mlp
       [Average Parallel L2 cache miss data reads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_mlp
       [Average Parallel L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD\,cmask\=1@]

Memory_Lat: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD@]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l3_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L3 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.L3_MISS_DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.L3_MISS_DEMAND_DATA_RD@]

MicroSeq: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_pipeline_ipassist
       [Instructions per a microcode Assist invocation]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@ASSISTS.ANY@]
       [tma_info_pipeline_ipassist < 100e3]
  tma_info_pipeline_strings_cycles
       [Estimated fraction of retirement-cycles dealing with repeat
        instructions]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.REP_ITERATION@ / cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS\,
        cmask\=1@]
       [tma_info_pipeline_strings_cycles > 0.1]
  tma_microcode_sequencer
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was retiring uops
        fetched by the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit]
       [cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS@ / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_ms
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit - see
        Microcode_Sequencer node for details]
       [max(cpu_core@IDQ.MS_CYCLES_ANY@,cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@)) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2.4]
       [tma_ms > 0.05 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [3 * cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.MS\,cmask\=1\,edge@ /
        (cpu_core@UOPS_RETIRED.SLOTS@ / cpu_core@UOPS_ISSUED.ANY@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

OS: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_system_ipfarbranch
       [Instructions per Far Branch ( Far Branches apply upon transition from
        application to operating system,handling interrupts,exceptions) [lower
        number means higher occurrence rate]]
       [cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@ / cpu_core@BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH@u]
       [tma_info_system_ipfarbranch < 1e6]
  tma_info_system_kernel_cpi
       [Cycles Per Instruction for the Operating System (OS) Kernel mode]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P@k / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@k]
  tma_info_system_kernel_utilization
       [Fraction of cycles spent in the Operating System (OS) Kernel mode]
       [cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P@k /
        cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@]
       [tma_info_system_kernel_utilization > 0.05]

Offcore: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Bandwidth related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_bandwidth / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_sq_full /
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_fb_full / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency
       [Total pipeline cost of external Memory- or Cache-Latency related
        bottlenecks]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_dram_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_mem_latency / (tma_mem_bandwidth + tma_mem_latency)) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_l3_hit_latency
        / (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency +
        tma_sq_full)) + tma_memory_bound * tma_l2_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) +
        tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound +
        tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) *
        (tma_l1_latency_dependency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_lock_latency / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_loads / (tma_dtlb_load + tma_fb_full +
        tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency + tma_split_loads +
        tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_split_stores / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)) + tma_memory_bound * (tma_store_bound /
        (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_store_latency / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Address Translation related bottlenecks
        (data-side TLBs)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l1_bound / max(tma_memory_bound,
        tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound +
        tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_load / max(tma_l1_bound,tma_dtlb_load +
        tma_fb_full + tma_l1_latency_dependency + tma_lock_latency +
        tma_split_loads + tma_store_fwd_blk)) + tma_memory_bound *
        (tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound)) * (tma_dtlb_store / (tma_dtlb_store +
        tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores)))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs > 20]
  tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization
       [Total pipeline cost of Memory Synchronization related bottlenecks
        (data transfers and coherency updates across processors)]
       [100 * (tma_memory_bound * (tma_l3_bound / (tma_dram_bound +
        tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound + tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) *
        (tma_contested_accesses + tma_data_sharing) / (tma_contested_accesses
        + tma_data_sharing + tma_l3_hit_latency + tma_sq_full) +
        tma_store_bound / (tma_dram_bound + tma_l1_bound + tma_l2_bound +
        tma_l3_bound + tma_store_bound) * tma_false_sharing / (tma_dtlb_store
        + tma_false_sharing + tma_split_stores + tma_store_latency +
        tma_streaming_stores - tma_store_latency)) + tma_machine_clears * (1 -
        tma_other_nukes / tma_other_nukes))]
       [tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization > 10]
  tma_bottleneck_other_bottlenecks
       [Total pipeline cost of remaining bottlenecks in the back-end]
       [100 - (tma_bottleneck_big_code + tma_bottleneck_instruction_fetch_bw +
        tma_bottleneck_mispredictions +
        tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_bandwidth +
        tma_bottleneck_data_cache_memory_latency +
        tma_bottleneck_memory_data_tlbs +
        tma_bottleneck_memory_synchronization +
        tma_bottleneck_compute_bound_est + tma_bottleneck_irregular_overhead +
        tma_bottleneck_branching_overhead + tma_bottleneck_useful_work)]
       [tma_bottleneck_other_bottlenecks > 20]
  tma_code_l2_hit
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that hit in the L2 cache]
       [max(0,tma_icache_misses - tma_code_l2_miss)]
       [tma_code_l2_hit > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_code_l2_miss
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses that miss in the L2 cache]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_CODE_RD@ /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_code_l2_miss > 0.05 & (tma_icache_misses > 0.05 &
        (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15))]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(25 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ *
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) + 24 *
        tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS@) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [24 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        (cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_NO_FWD@ +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_FWD@ * (1 -
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ /
        (cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ +
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_DATA_RD.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HIT_WITH_FWD@))) * (1 +
        cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.FB_HIT@ / cpu_core@MEM_LOAD_RETIRED.L1_MISS@
        / 2) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [28 * tma_info_system_core_frequency *
        cpu_core@OCR.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.SNOOP_HITM@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_access_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data access bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_access_bw]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_all
       [L2 cache ([RKL+] true) misses per kilo instruction for all request
        types (including speculative)]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_rfo
       [Offcore requests (L2 cache miss) per kilo instruction for demand RFOs]
       [1e3 * cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS@ / cpu_core@INST_RETIRED.ANY@]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_access_bw
       [Average per-thread data access bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.ALL_REQUESTS@ / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_latency_data_l2_mlp
       [Average Parallel L2 cache miss data reads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD@]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_mlp
       [Average Parallel L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD\,cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l3_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L3 cache miss demand Loads]
       [cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.L3_MISS_DEMAND_DATA_RD@ /
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS.L3_MISS_DEMAND_DATA_RD@]
  tma_lock_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling cache
        misses due to lock operations]
       [(16 * max(0,cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ -
        cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.ALL_RFO@) + cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS@ /
        cpu_core@MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@ * (10 *
        cpu_core@L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT@ + min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO@))) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lock_latency > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=4@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(cpu_core@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD@,
        cpu_core@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(cpu_core@XQ.FULL_CYCLES@ + cpu_core@L1D_PEND_MISS.L2_STALLS@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
