load_store_bound: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_load_miss_bound_%_loadmissbound_with_l2hit
       [Percentage of memory bound stalls where retirement is stalled due to
        an L1 miss that hit the L2]
       [100 * cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD_L2_HIT@ /
        cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD@]
  tma_info_load_miss_bound_%_loadmissbound_with_l2miss
       [Percentage of memory bound stalls where retirement is stalled due to
        an L1 miss that subsequently misses in the L2]
       [100 * (cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD_LLC_HIT@ +
        cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD_DRAM_HIT@) /
        cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD@]
  tma_info_load_miss_bound_%_loadmissbound_with_l3hit
       [Percentage of memory bound stalls where retirement is stalled due to
        an L1 miss that hit the L3]
       [100 * cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD_LLC_HIT@ /
        cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD@]
  tma_info_load_miss_bound_%_loadmissbound_with_l3miss
       [Percentage of memory bound stalls where retirement is stalled due to
        an L1 miss that subsequently misses the L3]
       [100 * cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD_DRAM_HIT@ /
        cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD@]
  tma_info_load_store_bound_l1_bound
       [Counts the number of cycles that the oldest load of the load buffer is
        stalled at retirement due to a pipeline block]
       [100 * cpu_atom@LD_HEAD.L1_BOUND_AT_RET@ /
        cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@]
  tma_info_load_store_bound_load_bound
       [Counts the number of cycles that the oldest load of the load buffer is
        stalled at retirement]
       [100 * (cpu_atom@LD_HEAD.L1_BOUND_AT_RET@ +
        cpu_atom@MEM_BOUND_STALLS.LOAD@) / cpu_atom@CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.CORE@]
  tma_info_load_store_bound_store_bound
       [Counts the number of cycles the core is stalled due to store buffer
        full]
       [100 * (cpu_atom@MEM_SCHEDULER_BLOCK.ST_BUF@ /
        cpu_atom@MEM_SCHEDULER_BLOCK.ALL@) * tma_mem_scheduler]

lpm_br: [breakdown of retired branch instructions]
  lpm_br_cond_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of not taken
        conditional branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN)]
  lpm_br_cond_mispred
       [Retired not taken conditional branch instructions mispredicted as a
        percentage of all not taken conditional branches]
       [d_ratio(BR_MISP_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN,BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN)]
  lpm_br_cond_retired
       [Retired conditional not taken branch instructions]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.COND_NTAKEN,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_far_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of far branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH)]
  lpm_br_far_retired
       [Retired far control transfers per second]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_taken_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of taken branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_taken_mispred
       [The number of retired taken branch instructions that were mispredicted
        as a percentage of all taken branches]
       [d_ratio(BR_MISP_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_taken_retired
       [The number of taken branches that were retired per second]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_total_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_total_insn_fe_resteers
       [The number of resync branches per second]
       [d_ratio(BACLEARS.ANY,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_total_mispred
       [The number of branch instructions retired,of any type,that were not

       [Branches taken per context switch]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN,context\-switches)]

       [Cycles per context switch]
       [d_ratio(cycles,context\-switches)]

       [Instructions per context switch]
       [d_ratio(instructions,context\-switches)]

       [L2 misses per context switch]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS +
        L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS + L2_RQSTS.HWPF_MISS,context\-switches)]

       [Loads per context switch]
       [d_ratio(MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS,context\-switches)]

       [Context switches per second]

       [Stores per context switch]
       [d_ratio(MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES,context\-switches)]

       [Hypervisor guest cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(cpu\-cycles:G,cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G +
        cpu\-cycles:uH)]

       [Kernel cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(cpu\-cycles:kHh,cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G +
        cpu\-cycles:uH)]

       [Total number of cycles]
       [cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G + cpu\-cycles:uH]
  lpm_cycles_user
       [User cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(cpu\-cycles:uH,cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G +
        cpu\-cycles:uH)]

       [d_ratio(FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_SINGLE +
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_DOUBLE + 4 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE + 2 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE + 8 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE + 4 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE,cycles)]
  lpm_fpu_total_flops
       [Floating point operations per second]
       [d_ratio(FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_SINGLE +
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.SCALAR_DOUBLE + 4 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_SINGLE + 2 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.128B_PACKED_DOUBLE + 8 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_SINGLE + 4 *
        FP_ARITH_INST_RETIRED.256B_PACKED_DOUBLE,duration_time)]

lpm_idle:
  lpm_idle
       [Percentage of total wallclock cycles where CPUs are in low power state
        (C1 or deeper sleep state)]
       [d_ratio(max(msr@tsc@ - msr@mperf@,0),msr@tsc@)]

lpm_ilp:
  lpm_ilp_idle
       [Lower power cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(msr@tsc@ - msr@mperf@,msr@tsc@)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_0
       [Instructions retired in 0 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [1 - d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=1@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED) -
        d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED) -
        d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED) -
        d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED) -
        d_ratio(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_1
       [Instructions retired in 1 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=1@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_2
       [Instructions retired in 2 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_3
       [Instructions retired in 3 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_4
       [Instructions retired in 4 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_5
       [Instructions retired in 5 or more cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED)]

lpm_l2: [L2 data cache analysis]
  lpm_l2_code_hits
       [L2 cache code hits]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT,L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT +
        L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS)]
  lpm_l2_code_misses
       [L2 cache code misses]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS,L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT +
        L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS)]
  lpm_l2_code_requests
       [L2 cache code requests per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT + L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rd_hits
       [L2 cache data read hits]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS,L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS +
        L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_HIT)]
  lpm_l2_rd_misses
       [L2 cache data read misses per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rd_requests
       [L2 cache data read requests per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS + L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_HIT,
        duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rfo_hits
       [L2 cache request for ownership (RFO) hits]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT,L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS)]
  lpm_l2_rfo_misses
       [L2 cache request for ownership (RFO) misses]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS,L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS)]
  lpm_l2_rfo_requests
       [L2 cache request for ownership (RFO) requests per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_totals_in
       [L2 cache total in per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_LINES_IN.ALL,duration_time)]

lpm_ldst: [Breakdown of load/store instructions]

        MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS@cmask\=2@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD)]

        MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS@cmask\=3@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD)]

        MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_STORES@cmask\=3@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD)]

  lpm_ldst_total_stores

        (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING,(L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES / 2 if

lpm_ports: [functional unit (port) utilization -- fraction of cycles each port is utilized (higher is better)]
  lpm_port_0
       [port_0 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_0,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2 if
        #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]
  lpm_port_1
       [port_1 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_1,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2 if
        #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]
  lpm_port_2_3_10
       [port_2_3_10 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_2_3_10,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2
        if #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]
  lpm_port_4_9
       [port_4_9 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_4_9,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2 if
        #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]
  lpm_port_5_11
       [port_5_11 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_5_11,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2 if
        #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]
  lpm_port_6
       [port_6 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_6,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2 if
        #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]
  lpm_port_7_8
       [port_7_8 utilization (higher is better)]
       [d_ratio(UOPS_DISPATCHED.PORT_7_8,(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED / 2 if
        #smt_on else CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.DISTRIBUTED))]

  lpm_swpf_bkdwn_nta_per_swpf

        instructions]

        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW)]

       [d_ratio(SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA,duration_time)]
  lpm_swpf_bkdwn_t0_per_swpf

        instructions]
       [d_ratio(SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T0,SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA +
        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T0 + SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T1_T2 +
        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW)]
  lpm_swpf_bkdwn_t0_rate

       [d_ratio(SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T0,duration_time)]
  lpm_swpf_bkdwn_t1_t2_per_swpf
       [Software prefetch T1 or T2 instructions as a percent of all prefetch
        instructions]
       [d_ratio(SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T1_T2,SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA +

        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW)]
  lpm_swpf_bkdwn_t1_t2_rate

  lpm_swpf_bkdwn_w_per_swpf

       [d_ratio(SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW,SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA +

  lpm_swpf_totals_exec

       [d_ratio(SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA + SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T0 +
        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T1_T2 + SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW,duration_time)]
  lpm_swpf_totals_insn_per_pf
       [Average number of instructions between software prefetches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA + SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T0 +
        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T1_T2 + SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW)]
  lpm_swpf_totals_loads_per_pf
       [Average number of loads between software prefetches]
       [d_ratio(MEM_INST_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS,SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.NTA +
        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T0 + SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.T1_T2 +
        SW_PREFETCH_ACCESS.PREFETCHW)]
